- **database-redis-rate-limiting**: Guides the agent on implementing rate limiters in Redis — a fixed-window counter (INCR/EXPIRE) for the simple case, and a sliding-window Sorted Set for accurate or weighted limiting — done atomically to avoid race conditions between the check and the increment. - **database-redis-replication-consistency**: Guides the agent on Redis's asynchronous replication model — why a replica can lag behind its master, what that means for data loss on failover, and the WAIT/WAITAOF commands for tightening the consistency guarantee at a real, measurable throughput cost — for deciding how much replication consistency a use case actually needs.
